




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


udānapāḷi


1. baudhivaggau

1. paṭhamabaudhisuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai baudhirukkhamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī vimuttisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)| atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumaṃ sādhukaṃ manasākāsi –

‘‘iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yadā  havai pātubhavanti dhammā,

ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

athassa kaṅkhā vapayanti sabbā,

yatau pajānāti sahaitudhamma’’nti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyabaudhisuttaṃ

2. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aika samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai baudhirukkhamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī| atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ paṭilaumaṃ sādhukaṃ manasākāsi –

‘‘iti imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā,

ātāpinau  jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

athassa kaṅkhā vapayanti sabbā,

yatau khayaṃ pac‍cayānaṃ avaidī’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyabaudhisuttaṃ

3. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai baudhirukkhamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| taina khau pana samayaina  bhagavā sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī| atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ sādhukaṃ manasākāsi –

‘‘iti imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā idaṃ uppaj‍jati, imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati; yadidaṃ – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau  hautī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā,

ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

vidhūpayaṃ tiṭṭhati mārasainaṃ,

sūriyauva suriyauva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aubhāsayamantalikkha’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. huṃhuṅkasuttaṃ

4. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai ajapālanigraudhai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī| atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhāsi|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau huṃhuṅkajātikau huhuṅkajātikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhau gautama, brāhmaṇau hauti, katamai ca pana brāhmaṇakaraṇā brāhmaṇakārakā (ka॰) dhammā’’ti?

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā  tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yau brāhmaṇau bāhitapāpadhammau,

nihuṃhuṅkau nihuhuṅkau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ pī॰) nik‍kasāvau yatattau|

vaidantagū vūsitabrahmacariyau,

dhammaina sau brahmavādaṃ vadaiyya|

yassussadā natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. brāhmaṇasuttaṃ

5. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmā ca mahākassapau āyasmā ca mahākac‍cānau mahākac‍cāyanau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmā ca mahākappinau āyasmā ca mahācundau āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca raivatau āyasmā ca nandau ānandau (sī॰ pī॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu|

addasā  khau bhagavā tai āyasmantai dūratauva āgacchantai; disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aitai, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā āgacchanti; aitai, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇā āgacchantī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau brāhmaṇajātikau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, brāhmaṇau hauti, katamai ca pana brāhmaṇakaraṇā dhammā’’ti?

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘bāhitvā pāpakai dhammai, yai caranti sadā satā|

khīṇasaṃyaujanā  buddhā, tai vai taiva (sī॰) laukasmi brāhmaṇā’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mahākassapasuttaṃ

6. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahākassapau pippaliguhāyaṃ pipphaliguhāyaṃ (syā॰), simbaliguhāyaṃ (ka॰) viharati ābādhikau ābādhikau hauti (syā॰ pī॰) dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau aparaina samayaina tamhā ābādhā vuṭṭhāsi| atha khau āyasmatau mahākassapassa tamhā ābādhā vuṭṭhitassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pavisaiyya’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina pañ‍camattāni daivatāsatāni ussuk‍kaṃ āpan‍nāni haunti āyasmatau mahākassapassa piṇḍapātapaṭilābhāya| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau tāni pañ‍camattāni daivatāsatāni paṭikkhipitvā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi – yaina daliddavisikhā kapaṇavisikhā paisakāravisikhā| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ rājagahai piṇḍāya carantaṃ yaina daliddavisikhā kapaṇavisikhā paisakāravisikhā|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘anañ‍ñapausimañ‍ñātaṃ, dantaṃ sārai patiṭṭhitaṃ|

khīṇāsavaṃ vantadausaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇa’’nti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ajakalāpakasuttaṃ

7. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā pāvāyaṃ pāṭaliyaṃ (pī॰) viharati ajakalāpakai  caitiyai, ajakalāpakassa yakkhassa bhavanai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā rattandhakāratimisāyaṃ  abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti; daivau ca aikamaikaṃ phusāyati| atha khau ajakalāpakau yakkhau bhagavatau bhayaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsaṃ uppādaitukāmau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau avidūrai tikkhattuṃ ‘‘ak‍kulau pak‍kulau’’ti ak‍kulapak‍kulikaṃ akāsi – ‘‘aisau tai, samaṇa, pisācau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yadā sakaisu dhammaisu, pāragū hauti brāhmaṇau|

atha aitaṃ pisācañ‍ca, pak‍kulañ‍cātivattatī’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. saṅgāmajisuttaṃ

8. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā saṅgāmaji sāvatthiṃ anuppattau hauti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| assausi khau āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa purāṇadutiyikā – ‘‘ayyau kira saṅgāmaji sāvatthiṃ anuppattau’’ti| sā dārakaṃ ādāya jaitavanaṃ agamāsi|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā saṅgāmaji añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa purāṇadutiyikā yaināyasmā saṅgāmaji tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ saṅgāmajiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘khuddaputtañhi khuddaputtāmhi (sī॰), samaṇa, pausa ma’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā saṅgāmaji tuṇhī ahausi|

dutiyampi khau āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa purāṇadutiyikā āyasmantaṃ  saṅgāmajiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘khuddaputtañhi, samaṇa, pausa ma’’nti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā saṅgāmaji tuṇhī ahausi|

tatiyampi  khau āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa purāṇadutiyikā āyasmantaṃ saṅgāmajiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘khuddaputtañhi, samaṇa, pausa ma’’nti| tatiyampi khau āyasmā saṅgāmaji tuṇhī ahausi|

atha khau āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa purāṇadutiyikā taṃ dārakaṃ āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa puratau nikkhipitvā pak‍kāmi pak‍kami (ka॰) aivamuparipi – ‘‘aisau aisa (sī॰ ka॰) tai, samaṇa, puttau; pausa na’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā saṅgāmaji taṃ dārakaṃ naiva aulaukaisi nāpi ālapi| atha khau āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa purāṇadutiyikā  avidūraṃ avidūrai (syā॰ pī॰) gantvā apalaukaintī addasa āyasmantaṃ saṅgāmajiṃ taṃ dārakaṃ naiva aulaukaintaṃ nāpi ālapantaṃ, disvānassā aitadahausi – ‘‘na cāyaṃ samaṇau puttainapi atthikau’’ti| tatau paṭinivattitvā dārakaṃ ādāya pak‍kāmi| addasā khau bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina āyasmatau saṅgāmajissa purāṇadutiyikāya aivarūpaṃ vippakāraṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘āyantiṃ nābhinandati, pak‍kamantiṃ na saucati|

saṅgā saṅgāmajiṃ muttaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇa’’nti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. jaṭilasuttaṃ

9. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā gayāyaṃ viharati gayāsīsai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā jaṭilā sītāsu haimantikāsu  rattīsu antaraṭṭhakai himapātasamayai gayāyaṃ ummuj‍jantipi nimuj‍jantipi, ummuj‍janimuj‍jampi karaunti ausiñ‍cantipi, aggimpi juhanti – ‘‘iminā suddhī’’ti|

addasā khau bhagavā tai sambahulai jaṭilai sītāsu haimantikāsu rattīsu antaraṭṭhakai himapātasamayai gayāyaṃ ummuj‍jantaipi nimuj‍jantaipi ummuj‍janimuj‍jampi karauntai ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karauntaipi (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) ausiñ‍cantaipi aggimpi juhantai – ‘‘iminā suddhī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘na udakaina sucī hautī, bahvaittha nhāyatī nahāyatī (sī॰) janau|

yamhi sac‍cañ‍ca dhammau ca, sau sucī sau ca brāhmaṇau’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. bāhiyasuttaṃ

10. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bāhiyau dārucīriyau suppārakai paṭivasati samuddatīrai sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| atha khau bāhiyassa dārucīriyassa rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘yai khau kaici laukai arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nā, ahaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatarau’’ti|

atha khau  bāhiyassa dārucīriyassa purāṇasālauhitā daivatā anukampikā atthakāmā bāhiyassa dārucīriyassa caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya yaina bāhiyau dārucīriyau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bāhiyaṃ dārucīriyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘naiva khau tvaṃ, bāhiya, arahā, nāpi arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nau| sāpi tai paṭipadā natthi yāya tvaṃ arahā vā assa assasi (syā॰ ka॰) arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nau’’ti|

‘‘atha kai carahi sadaivakai laukai arahantau vā arahattamaggaṃ vā samāpan‍nau’’ti? ‘‘atthi, bāhiya, uttaraisu janapadaisu janapadai (sī॰) sāvatthi nāma nagaraṃ| tattha sau bhagavā aitarahi viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| sau hi, bāhiya, bhagavā arahā caiva arahattāya ca dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti|

atha khau bāhiyau dārucīriyau tāya daivatāya saṃvaijitau tāvadaiva suppārakamhā pak‍kāmi| sabbattha aikarattiparivāsaina yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina  sambahulā bhikkhū abbhaukāsai caṅkamanti| atha khau bāhiyau dārucīriyau yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhantai, aitarahi bhagavā viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau? dassanakāmamhā mayaṃ taṃ  bhagavantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddha’’nti| ‘‘antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau khau, bāhiya, bhagavā piṇḍāyā’’ti|

atha khau bāhiyau dārucīriyau taramānarūpau jaitavanā nikkhamitvā sāvatthiṃ pavisitvā addasa bhagavantaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ pāsādikaṃ pasādanīyaṃ santindriyaṃ santamānasaṃ  uttamadamathasamathamanuppattaṃ dantaṃ guttaṃ yatindriyaṃ nāgaṃ| disvāna yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau pādai sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘daisaitu mai, bhantai bhagavā, dhammaṃ; daisaitu, sugatau, dhammaṃ, yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā bāhiyaṃ dārucīriyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘akālau khau tāva, bāhiya, antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhamhā piṇḍāyā’’ti|

dutiyampi khau bāhiyau dārucīriyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘duj‍jānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau vā jīvitantarāyānaṃ, mayhaṃ vā jīvitantarāyānaṃ| daisaitu mai, bhantai bhagavā, dhammaṃ; daisaitu, sugatau, dhammaṃ, yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā bāhiyaṃ dārucīriyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘akālau khau tāva, bāhiya, antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhamhā piṇḍāyā’’ti|

tatiyampi khau bāhiyau dārucīriyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘duj‍jānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau vā jīvitantarāyānaṃ, mayhaṃ vā jīvitantarāyānaṃ| daisaitu mai bhantai bhagavā, dhammaṃ; daisaitu, sugatau, dhammaṃ, yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha tai, bāhiya, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘diṭṭhai diṭṭhamattaṃ bhavissati, sutai sutamattaṃ bhavissati, mutai mutamattaṃ bhavissati, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātamattaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, bāhiya, sikkhitabbaṃ| yatau khau tai, bāhiya, diṭṭhai diṭṭhamattaṃ bhavissati, sutai sutamattaṃ bhavissati, mutai  mutamattaṃ bhavissati, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātamattaṃ bhavissati, tatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, na taina; yatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, na taina tatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, na tattha; yatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, na tattha, tatau tvaṃ, bāhiya, naividha na huraṃ na ubhayamantaraina| aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti|

atha khau bāhiyassa dārucīriyassa bhagavatau imāya saṃkhittāya dhammadaisanāya tāvadaiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍ci|

atha  khau bhagavā bāhiyaṃ dārucīriyaṃ iminā saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvaditvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau bāhiyaṃ dārucīriyaṃ gāvī taruṇavacchā adhipatitvā adhipātaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), adhipātitvā (ka॰) jīvitā vauraupaisi|

atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ nagaramhā nikkhamitvā addasa bāhiyaṃ dārucīriyaṃ kālaṅkataṃ kālakataṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰); disvāna bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘gaṇhatha, bhikkhavai, bāhiyassa dārucīriyassa sarīrakaṃ; mañ‍cakaṃ āraupaitvā nīharitvā jhāpaitha; thūpañ‍cassa karautha| sabrahmacārī vau, bhikkhavai, kālaṅkatau’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā bāhiyassa dārucīriyassa sarīrakaṃ mañ‍cakaṃ āraupaitvā nīharitvā jhāpaitvā thūpañ‍cassa katvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘daḍḍhaṃ, bhantai, bāhiyassa dārucīriyassa sarīraṃ, thūpau cassa  katau| tassa kā gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’ti? ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, bāhiyau dārucīriyau pac‍capādi dhammassānudhammaṃ; na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ  vihaisaisi| parinibbutau, bhikkhavai, bāhiyau dārucīriyau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yattha āpau ca pathavī, taijau vāyau na gādhati|

na tattha suk‍kā jautanti, ādic‍cau nappakāsati|

na tattha candimā bhāti, tamau tattha na vij‍jati||

‘‘yadā  ca attanāvaidi vaidhī (ka॰), muni maunaina brāhmaṇau|

atha rūpā arūpā ca, sukhadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

(ayampi udānau vuttau bhagavatā iti mai sutanti|) ( ) syāmapautthakai natthi

baudhivaggau paṭhamau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

tayau baudhi ca huṃhuṅkau tayau ca baudhi nigraudhau (sabbattha), brāhmaṇau tai thairā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), thairau (ka॰) kassapaina ca|

aja pāvāya (sī॰ syā॰), pāṭaliyaṃ (pī॰), pāvā (ka॰) saṅgāma jaṭilā, bāhiyaināti tai dasāti||


2. mucalindavaggau

1. mucalindasuttaṃ

11. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai mucalindamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī|

taina khau pana samayaina mahā akālamaighau udapādi  sattāhavaddalikā sītavātaduddinī| atha khau mucalindau nāgarājā sakabhavanā nikkhamitvā bhagavatau kāyaṃ sattakkhattuṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvā uparimuddhani mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ vihac‍ca aṭṭhāsi – ‘‘mā bhagavantaṃ sītaṃ, mā bhagavantaṃ uṇhaṃ, mā bhagavantaṃ ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapa siriṃsapa (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samphassau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhāsi| atha khau mucalindau nāgarājā viddhaṃ vigatavalāhakaṃ daivaṃ viditvā bhagavatau kāyā bhaugai vinivaiṭhaitvā sakavaṇṇaṃ paṭisaṃharitvā māṇavakavaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā bhagavatau puratau aṭṭhāsi pañ‍jalikau bhagavantaṃ namassamānau|

atha  khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sukhau vivaikau tuṭṭhassa, sutadhammassa passatau|

abyāpaj‍jaṃ sukhaṃ laukai, pāṇabhūtaisu saṃyamau||

‘‘sukhā  virāgatā laukai, kāmānaṃ samatik‍kamau|

asmimānassa yau vinayau, aitaṃ vai paramaṃ sukha’’nti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. rājasuttaṃ

12. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ  upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau, imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ rājūnaṃ mahaddhanatarau vā mahābhaugatarau vā mahākausatarau vā mahāvijitatarau vā mahāvāhanatarau vā  mahabbalatarau vā mahiddhikatarau vā mahānubhāvatarau vā rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau, rājā vā pasainadi kausalau’’ti? ayañ‍carahi taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā hauti vippakatā|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā san‍nipatitā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘kau nu khau, āvusau, imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ rājūnaṃ mahaddhanatarau vā mahābhaugatarau vā mahākausatarau vā mahāvijitatarau vā mahāvāhanatarau vā mahabbalatarau vā mahiddhikatarau vā mahānubhāvatarau vā rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau, rājā vā pasainadi kausalau’ti? ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

‘‘na  khvaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ yaṃ tumhai aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ kathaiyyātha| san‍nipatitānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ – dhammī vā kathā ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yañ‍ca  kāmasukhaṃ laukai, yañ‍cidaṃ diviyaṃ sukhaṃ|

taṇhakkhayasukhassaitai, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasi’’nti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. daṇḍasuttaṃ

13. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā kumārakā antarā ca sāvatthiṃ antarā ca jaitavanaṃ ahiṃ daṇḍaina hananti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā khau bhagavā sambahulai kumārakai antarā ca sāvatthiṃ antarā ca jaitavanaṃ ahiṃ daṇḍaina hanantai|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sukhakāmāni bhūtāni, yau daṇḍaina vihiṃsati|

attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau na labhatai sukhaṃ||

‘‘sukhakāmāni bhūtāni, yau daṇḍaina na hiṃsati|

attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau labhatai sukha’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. sak‍kārasuttaṃ

14. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sak‍katau hauti garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau, lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| bhikkhusaṅghaupi sak‍katau hauti  garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau, lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| añ‍ñatitthiyā pana paribbājakā asak‍katā haunti agarukatā amānitā na apacitā (syā॰ pī॰) apūjitā  anapacitā, na lābhinau cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| atha khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā bhagavatau sak‍kāraṃ asahamānā bhikkhusaṅghassa ca gāmai ca arañ‍ñai ca bhikkhū disvā asabbhāhi pharusāhi vācāhi ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti rausainti vihaisainti|

atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aitarahi, bhantai, bhagavā sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau, lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| bhikkhusaṅghaupi sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau, lābhī cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| añ‍ñatitthiyā pana paribbājakā asak‍katā agarukatā amānitā apūjitā anapacitā, na lābhinau cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| atha khau tai, bhantai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā bhagavatau sak‍kāraṃ asahamānā bhikkhusaṅghassa ca gāmai ca arañ‍ñai ca bhikkhū disvā asabbhāhi pharusāhi vācāhi ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti rausainti vihaisantī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘gāmai arañ‍ñai sukhadukkhaphuṭṭhau,

naivattatau nau paratau dahaitha|

phusanti  phassā upadhiṃ paṭic‍ca,

nirūpadhiṃ kaina phusaiyyu phassā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. upāsakasuttaṃ

15. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau icchānaṅgalakau  upāsakau sāvatthiṃ anuppattau hauti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau sau upāsakau sāvatthiyaṃ taṃ karaṇīyaṃ tīraitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ upāsakaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘cirassaṃ khau tvaṃ, upāsaka, imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāyā’’ti|

‘‘cirapaṭikāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitukāmau, api cāhaṃ kaihici kaihici kic‍cakaraṇīyaihi byāvaṭau| aivāhaṃ nāsakkhiṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitu’’nti|

atha  khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sukhaṃ vata tassa na hauti kiñ‍ci,

saṅkhātadhammassa bahussutassa|

sakiñ‍canaṃ passa vihañ‍ñamānaṃ,

janau janasmiṃ paṭibandharūpau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. gabbhinīsuttaṃ

16. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa paribbājakassa daharamāṇavikā pajāpati hauti gabbhinī  upavijañ‍ñā| atha khau sā paribbājikā taṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, tailaṃ āhara, yaṃ mai vijātāya bhavissatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, sau paribbājakau taṃ paribbājikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kutau panāhaṃ, bhauti bhautiyā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), tailaṃ āharāmī’’ti? dutiyampi khau sā paribbājikā taṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, tailaṃ āhara, yaṃ mai vijātāya bhavissatī’’ti| dutiyampi khau sau paribbājikau taṃ paribbājikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kutau panāhaṃ, bhauti, tailaṃ āharāmī’’ti? tatiyampi khau sā paribbājikā taṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gaccha tvaṃ, brāhmaṇa, tailaṃ āhara, yaṃ mai vijātāya bhavissatī’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina rañ‍ñau  pasainadissa kausalassa kauṭṭhāgārai samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā sappissa vā tailassa vā yāvadatthaṃ pātuṃ dīyati diyyati (sī॰ ka॰), nau nīharituṃ|

atha khau tassa paribbājakassa aitadahausi – ‘‘rañ‍ñau khau pana pasainadissa kausalassa kauṭṭhāgārai samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā sappissa vā tailassa vā yāvadatthaṃ pātuṃ dīyati, nau nīharituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa kauṭṭhāgāraṃ gantvā tailassa yāvadatthaṃ pivitvā gharaṃ āgantvā ucchadditvāna uggiritvāna (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), ucchaditvā (sī॰ syā॰ aṭṭha॰), ucchaḍḍitvāna (ka॰) dadaiyyaṃ, yaṃ imissā vijātāya bhavissatī’’ti|

atha  khau sau paribbājakau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa kauṭṭhāgāraṃ gantvā tailassa yāvadatthaṃ pivitvā gharaṃ āgantvā naiva  sak‍kauti uddhaṃ kātuṃ, na pana adhau| sau dukkhāhi tibbāhi tippāhi (syā॰) kharāhi kaṭukāhi vaidanāhi phuṭṭhau āvaṭṭati parivaṭṭati|

atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā khau bhagavā taṃ paribbājakaṃ dukkhāhi tibbāhi kharāhi kaṭukāhi vaidanāhi phuṭṭhaṃ āvaṭṭamānaṃ parivaṭṭamānaṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sukhinau vata yai akiñ‍canā,

vaidagunau hi janā akiñ‍canā|

sakiñ‍canaṃ passa vihañ‍ñamānaṃ,

janau janasmiṃ paṭibandhacittau’’ paṭibaddhacittau (syā॰), paṭibandharupau (?) ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. aikaputtakasuttaṃ

17. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa upāsakassa aikaputtakau piyau manāpau kālaṅkatau hauti|

atha  khau sambahulā upāsakā al‍lavatthā al‍lakaisā divā divassa yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai upāsakai bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau tumhai, upāsakā, al‍lavatthā al‍lakaisā idhūpasaṅkamantā divā divassā’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai, sau upāsakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhaṃ khau, bhantai, aikaputtakau piyau manāpau  kālaṅkatau| taina mayaṃ al‍lavatthā al‍lakaisā idhūpasaṅkamantā divā divassā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘piyarūpassādagadhitāsai  piyarūpassātagadhitāsai (sī॰ pī॰),

daivakāyā puthu manussā ca|

aghāvinau parijun‍nā,

mac‍curājassa vasaṃ gacchanti||

‘‘yai vai divā ca rattau ca,

appamattā jahanti piyarūpaṃ|

tai vai khaṇanti aghamūlaṃ,

mac‍cunau āmisaṃ durativatta’’nti|| sattamaṃ|

8. suppavāsāsuttaṃ

18. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kuṇḍikāyaṃ kuṇḍiyāyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) viharati kuṇḍadhānavanai kuṇḍiṭṭhānavanai (syā॰ pī॰)| taina khau pana samayaina suppavāsā kauliyadhītā satta vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraiti| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā sā dukkhāhi tibbāhi kharāhi kaṭukāhi vaidanāhi phuṭṭhā tīhi vitak‍kaihi adhivāsaiti – ‘‘sammāsambuddhau vata sau bhagavā yau imassa aivarūpassa dukkhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti; suppaṭipan‍nau vata tassa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau yau imassa aivarūpassa dukkhassa pahānāya paṭipan‍nau; susukhaṃ vata taṃ nibbānaṃ yatthidaṃ aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ na saṃvij‍jatī’’ti|

atha  khau suppavāsā kauliyadhītā sāmikaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ayyaputta, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhi; appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ puccha – ‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati; appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā satta vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraiti| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā sā dukkhāhi tibbāhi kharāhi kaṭukāhi vaidanāhi phuṭṭhā tīhi vitak‍kaihi adhivāsaiti – sammāsambuddhau vata sau bhagavā yau imassa aivarūpassa dukkhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti; suppaṭipan‍nau vata tassa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau yau imassa aivarūpassa dukkhassa pahānāya paṭipan‍nau; susukhaṃ vata taṃ nibbānaṃ yatthidaṃ aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ na saṃvij‍jatī’’’ti|

‘‘parama’’nti khau sau kauliyaputtau suppavāsāya kauliyadhītāya paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau kauliyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati, appābādhaṃ appātaṅkaṃ lahuṭṭhānaṃ balaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ pucchati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā satta vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraiti| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā  sā dukkhāhi tibbāhi kharāhi kaṭukāhi vaidanāhi phuṭṭhā tīhi vitak‍kaihi adhivāsaiti – sammāsambuddhau vata sau bhagavā yau  imassa aivarūpassa dukkhassa pahānāya dhammaṃ daisaiti; suppaṭipan‍nau vata tassa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau yau imassa aivarūpassa dukkhassa pahānāya paṭipan‍nau; susukhaṃ vata nibbānaṃ yatthidaṃ aivarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ na saṃvij‍jatī’’’ti|

‘‘sukhinī hautu suppavāsā kauliyadhītā; araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijāyatū’’ti| saha vacanā ca pana bhagavatau suppavāsā kauliyadhītā sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijāyi|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau kauliyaputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina sakaṃ gharaṃ taina pac‍cāyāsi| addasā khau sau kauliyaputtau suppavāsaṃ kauliyadhītaraṃ sukhiniṃ araugaṃ araugaṃ  puttaṃ vijātaṃ| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau, tathāgatassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāmāyaṃ suppavāsā kauliyadhītā saha vacanā ca pana saha vacanā pana (pī॰), saha vacanā (?) bhagavatau sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijāyissatī’’ti! attamanau pamuditau pītisaumanassajātau ahausi|

atha khau suppavāsā kauliyadhītā sāmikaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, ayyaputta, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandāhi – ‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’ti; aivañ‍ca vadaihi – ‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā satta vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraiti| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā sā aitarahi sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijātā| sā sattāhaṃ buddhappamukhaṃ  bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhattaina nimantaiti| adhivāsaitu kira, bhantai, bhagavā suppavāsāya kauliyadhītāya satta bhattāni saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’’ti|

‘‘parama’’nti khau sau kauliyaputtau suppavāsāya kauliyadhītāya paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau kauliyaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘suppavāsā, bhantai, kauliyadhītā satta vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraiti| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā sā aitarahi sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijātā| sā sattāhaṃ buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhattaina nimantaiti| adhivāsaitu kira, bhantai, bhagavā suppavāsāya kauliyadhītāya satta bhattāni saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñataraina upāsakaina buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau svātanāya bhattaina nimantitau hauti| sau ca upāsakau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa mahāmauggalānassa (ka॰) upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, yaina sau upāsakau tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ upāsakaṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘suppavāsā, āvusau, kauliyadhītā satta  vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraisi| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā sā aitarahi sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijātā| sā sattāhaṃ buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhattaina  nimantaiti| karautu suppavāsā kauliyadhītā satta bhattāni, pacchā tvaṃ karissasī’ti karissasīti sañ‍ñāpaihi (ka॰)| tuyhaisau upaṭṭhākau’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina sau upāsakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘suppavāsā, āvusau, kauliyadhītā satta vassāni gabbhaṃ dhāraiti| sattāhaṃ mūḷhagabbhā sā aitarahi sukhinī araugā araugaṃ puttaṃ vijātā| sā sattāhaṃ buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhattaina nimantaiti| karautu suppavāsā kauliyadhītā satta bhattāni, pacchā tvaṃ karissasī’’ti|

‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, ayyau mahāmauggal‍lānau tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ pāṭibhaugau – bhaugānañ‍ca jīvitassa ca saddhāya ca, karautu suppavāsā kauliyadhītā satta bhattāni, pacchāhaṃ karissāmī’’ti| ‘‘dvin‍naṃ khau tai ahaṃ dvin‍naṃ khau taisaṃ (pī॰), dvin‍naṃ khau naisaṃ (ka॰), āvusau, dhammānaṃ pāṭibhaugau – bhaugānañ‍ca jīvitassa ca| saddhāya pana tvaṃyaiva pāṭibhaugau’’ti|

‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, ayyau mahāmauggal‍lānau dvin‍naṃ dhammānaṃ pāṭibhaugau – bhaugānañ‍ca jīvitassa ca, karautu suppavāsā kauliyadhītā satta bhattāni, pacchāhaṃ karissāmī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau taṃ upāsakaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sañ‍ñattau sañ‍ñātau (syā॰), bhantai, sau upāsakau mayā; karautu suppavāsā kauliyadhītā satta bhattāni, pacchā sau karissatī’’ti|

atha khau suppavāsā kauliyadhītā sattāhaṃ buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi  sampavāraisi, tañ‍ca dārakaṃ bhagavantaṃ vandāpaisi sabbañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghaṃ|

atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau taṃ dārakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, dāraka, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci na kiñ‍ci dukkha’’nti? ‘‘kutau mai, bhantai sāriputta, khamanīyaṃ, kutau yāpanīyaṃ! satta mai vassāni lauhitakumbhiyaṃ vuttānī’’ti|

atha khau suppavāsā kauliyadhītā – ‘‘puttau mai dhammasaināpatinā saddhiṃ mantaitī’’ti attamanā pamuditā pītisaumanassajātā ahausi| atha khau bhagavā (suppavāsaṃ kaulīyadhītaraṃ attamanaṃ pamuditaṃ pītisaumanassajātaṃ viditvā disvā (sī॰)) ( ) natthi iṅgalisapautthakai suppavāsaṃ kauliyadhītaraṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchaiyyāsi tvaṃ, suppavāsai, añ‍ñampi aivarūpaṃ putta’’nti? ‘‘icchaiyyāmahaṃ, bhagavā, añ‍ñānipi aivarūpāni satta puttānī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘asātaṃ sātarūpaina, piyarūpaina appiyaṃ|

dukkhaṃ sukhassa rūpaina, pamattamativattatī’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. visākhāsuttaṃ

19. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| taina khau pana samayaina visākhāya migāramātuyā kaucidaiva atthau rañ‍ñai pasainadimhi kausalai paṭibaddhau paṭibandhau (pī॰ ka॰) hauti| taṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau na yathādhippāyaṃ tīraiti|

atha khau visākhā migāramātā divā divassa divādivassaiva (syā॰), divādivassaiyaiva (pī॰), divā divassayaiva (ka॰) yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ bhagavā  aitadavauca – ‘‘handa kutau nu tvaṃ, visākhai, āgacchasi divā divassā’’ti? ‘‘idha mai, bhantai, kaucidaiva atthau rañ‍ñai pasainadimhi kausalai paṭibaddhau; taṃ rājā pasainadi kausalau na yathādhippāyaṃ tīraitī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sabbaṃ paravasaṃ dukkhaṃ, sabbaṃ issariyaṃ sukhaṃ|

sādhāraṇai vihañ‍ñanti, yaugā hi duratik‍kamā’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. bhaddiyasuttaṃ

20. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā anupiyāyaṃ viharati ambavanai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā bhaddiyau kāḷīgaudhāya puttau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’nti!

assausuṃ khau sambahulā bhikkhū āyasmatau bhaddiyassa kāḷīgaudhāya puttassa arañ‍ñagatassapi rukkhamūlagatassapi suñ‍ñāgāragatassapi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaintassa – ‘‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’nti! sutvāna naisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘nissaṃsayaṃ khau, āvusau, āyasmā bhaddiyau kāḷīgaudhāya puttau anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carati, yaṃsa pubbai agāriyabhūtassa agārikabhūtassa (syā॰) raj‍jasukhaṃ, sau tamanussaramānau arañ‍ñagataupi  rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi  abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti!

atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, bhaddiyau kāḷīgaudhāya puttau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’nti! nissaṃsayaṃ khau, bhantai, āyasmā bhaddiyau kāḷīgaudhāya puttau anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carati| yaṃsa pubbai agāriyabhūtassa raj‍jasukhaṃ, sau tamanussaramānau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti!

atha  khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina bhaddiyaṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau bhaddiya, āmantaitī’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā bhaddiyau kāḷīgaudhāya puttau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhaddiyaṃ kāḷīgaudhāya puttaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau bhaddiya, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā bhaddiyau kāḷīgaudhāya puttau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ bhaddiyaṃ kāḷīgaudhāya puttaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, bhaddiya, arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti! ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ pana kaṃ pana (syā pī॰) tvaṃ, bhaddiya, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi  abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti! ‘‘pubbai mai, bhantai, agāriyabhūtassa raj‍jaṃ kāraintassa antaupi antaipurai rakkhā susaṃvihitā ahausi, bahipi antaipurai rakkhā susaṃvihitā ahausi, antaupi nagarai rakkhā susaṃvihitā ahausi, bahipi nagarai rakkhā susaṃvihitā ahausi, antaupi janapadai rakkhā susaṃvihitā ahausi, bahipi janapadai rakkhā susaṃvihitā ahausi| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ rakkhitau gaupitau santau bhītau ubbiggau ussaṅkī utrāsī vihāsiṃ| aitarahi khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi aikau aikakau (syā॰ pī॰) abhītau anubbiggau anussaṅkī anutrāsī appaussuk‍kau pan‍nalaumau paradattavuttau paradavuttau (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), migabhūtaina caitasā viharāmi| imaṃ idaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) khau ahaṃ, bhantai, atthavasaṃ sampassamānau arañ‍ñagataupi  rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi abhikkhaṇaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi udānaimi (ka॰) – ‘ahau sukhaṃ, ahau sukha’’’nti!

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yassantaratau  na santi kaupā,

itibhavābhavatañ‍ca vītivattau|

taṃ  vigatabhayaṃ sukhiṃ asaukaṃ,

daivā nānubhavanti dassanāyā’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

mucalindavaggau dutiyau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ –

mucalindau  rājā daṇḍaina, sak‍kārau upāsakaina ca|

gabbhinī aikaputtau ca, suppavāsā visākhā ca|

kāḷīgaudhāya bhaddiyauti||


3. nandavaggau

1. kammavipākajasuttaṃ

21. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya purāṇakammavipākajaṃ dukkhaṃ tibbaṃ kharaṃ kaṭukaṃ vaidanaṃ adhivāsaintau satau sampajānau avihañ‍ñamānau|

addasā khau bhagavā taṃ bhikkhuṃ avidūrai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya purāṇakammavipākajaṃ dukkhaṃ tibbaṃ kharaṃ kaṭukaṃ vaidanaṃ adhivāsaintaṃ sataṃ sampajānaṃ avihañ‍ñamānaṃ|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sabbakammajahassa bhikkhunau,

dhunamānassa purai kataṃ rajaṃ|

amamassa  ṭhitassa tādinau,

atthau natthi janaṃ lapaitavai’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. nandasuttaṃ

22. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā nandau bhagavatau bhātā mātucchāputtau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivamāraucaiti – ‘‘anabhiratau ahaṃ, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ carāmi; na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’ti|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, nandau bhagavatau bhātā mātucchāputtau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivamāraucaiti – ‘anabhiratau ahaṃ, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ carāmi, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya  hīnāyāvattissāmī’’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina nandaṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau nanda, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā nandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau nanda, āmantaitī’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā nandau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, nanda, sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivamāraucaisi – ‘anabhiratau ahaṃ, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ carāmi, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘kissa pana tvaṃ, nanda, anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carasi, na sak‍kausi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissasī’’ti? ‘‘sākiyānī maṃ mama (syā॰, aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā), bhantai, janapadakalyāṇī gharā nikkhamantassa nikkhamantaṃ (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) upaḍḍhul‍likhitaihi kaisaihi apalaukaitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘tuvaṭaṃ khau, ayyaputta, āgacchaiyyāsī’ti| sau  khau ahaṃ, bhantai, tamanussaramānau anabhiratau brahmacariyaṃ carāmi, na sak‍kaumi brahmacariyaṃ sandhāraituṃ, sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissāmī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ sammiñ‍jitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya sammiñ‍jaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), aivamaiva – jaitavanai antarahitau daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu pāturahausi|

taina khau pana samayaina pañ‍camattāni accharāsatāni sak‍kassa daivānamindassa upaṭṭhānaṃ āgatāni haunti kakuṭapādāni| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘passasi nau tvaṃ, nanda, imāni pañ‍ca accharāsatāni kakuṭapādānī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, nanda, katamā nu khau abhirūpatarā vā dassanīyatarā vā pāsādikatarā vā, sākiyānī vā janapadakalyāṇī, imāni vā pañ‍ca accharāsatāni kakuṭapādānī’’ti? ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, paluṭṭhamak‍kaṭī kaṇṇanāsacchin‍nā, aivamaiva khau, bhantai, sākiyānī janapadakalyāṇī  imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ accharāsatānaṃ  upanidhāya saṅkhyampi saṅkhampi (sī॰) naupaiti kalabhāgampi naupaiti upanidhimpi naupaiti| atha khau imāni pañ‍ca accharāsatāni abhirūpatarāni caiva dassanīyatarāni ca pāsādikatarāni cā’’ti|

‘‘abhirama, nanda, abhirama, nanda! ahaṃ tai pāṭibhaugau pañ‍can‍naṃ accharāsatānaṃ paṭilābhāya kakuṭapādāna’’nti| ‘‘sacai mai, bhantai, bhagavā pāṭibhaugau pañ‍can‍naṃ accharāsatānaṃ paṭilābhāya kakuṭapādānaṃ, abhiramissāmahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavati brahmacariyai’’ti bhagavā brahmacariyaiti (syā॰ pī॰), bhagavā brahmacariyanti (ka॰)|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ bāhāyaṃ gahaitvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu antarahitau jaitavanai pāturahausi|

assausuṃ  khau bhikkhū – ‘‘āyasmā kira nandau bhagavatau bhātā mātucchāputtau accharānaṃ  haitu brahmacariyaṃ carati; bhagavā kirassa pāṭibhaugau pañ‍can‍naṃ accharāsatānaṃ paṭilābhāya kakuṭapādāna’’nti|

atha khau āyasmatau nandassa sahāyakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ bhatakavādaina ca upak‍kitakavādaina ca samudācaranti – ‘‘bhatakau kirāyasmā nandau upak‍kitakau kirāyasmā nandau accharānaṃ haitu brahmacariyaṃ carati; bhagavā kirassa pāṭibhaugau pañ‍can‍naṃ accharāsatānaṃ paṭilābhāya kakuṭapādāna’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā nandau  sahāyakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bhatakavādaina ca upak‍kitakavādaina ca aṭṭīyamānau harāyamānau jigucchamānau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā nandau arahataṃ ahausi|

atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, nandau bhagavatau bhātā mātucchāputtau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti| bhagavataupi khau ñāṇaṃ udapādi – ‘‘nandau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā nandau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā nandau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaṃ mai, bhantai, bhagavā pāṭibhaugau pañ‍can‍naṃ accharāsatānaṃ paṭilābhāya kakuṭapādānaṃ, muñ‍cāmahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ aitasmā paṭissavā’’ti| ‘‘mayāpi khau  tvaṃ, nanda khau tai nanda (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), khau nanda (ka॰), caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau – ‘nandau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| daivatāpi mai aitamatthaṃ āraucaisi – ‘āyasmā, bhantai, nandau bhagavatau bhātā mātucchāputtau āsavānaṃ khayā  anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’ti| yadaiva khau tai, nanda, anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuttaṃ, athāhaṃ muttau aitasmā paṭissavā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yassa nittiṇṇau paṅkau,

madditau kāmakaṇṭakau|

mauhakkhayaṃ anuppattau,

sukhadukkhaisu na vaidhatī sa bhikkhū’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. yasaujasuttaṃ

23. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina yasaujappamukhāni pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni sāvatthiṃ anuppattāni haunti bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| taidha khau āgantukā bhikkhū naivāsikaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudamānā saināsanāni pañ‍ñāpayamānā pattacīvarāni paṭisāmayamānā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā uc‍cāsaddamahāsaddā (ka॰) ahaisuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘kai panaitai, ānanda, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? ‘‘aitāni, bhantai, yasaujappamukhāni pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni sāvatthiṃ anuppattāni bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| taitai āgantukā bhikkhū naivāsikaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudamānā saināsanāni pañ‍ñāpayamānā pattacīvarāni paṭisāmayamānā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā’’ti| ‘‘tainahānanda, mama vacanaina tai bhikkhū āmantaihi – ‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kiṃ nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā, kaivaṭṭā mañ‍ñai macchavilaupai’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā yasaujau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘imāni, bhantai, pañ‍camattāni bhikkhusatāni sāvatthiṃ anuppattāni bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| taimai āgantukā bhikkhū naivāsikaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ paṭisammaudamānā saināsanāni pañ‍ñāpayamānā pattacīvarāni paṭisāmayamānā uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā’’ti| ‘‘gacchatha, bhikkhavai, paṇāmaimi  vau vau paṇāmaimi (sabbattha) ma॰ ni॰ 2.157 passitabbaṃ; na vau mama santikai vatthabba’’nti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau paṭissutvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā paṭisaṃsāmaitvā (syā॰) pattacīvaramādāya yaina vaj‍jī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu| vaj‍jīsu anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānā yaina vaggumudā nadī tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā vaggumudāya nadiyā tīrai paṇṇakuṭiyau karitvā vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu|

atha khau āyasmā yasaujau vassūpagatau vassūpagatai (ka॰) bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhagavatā mayaṃ, āvusau, paṇāmitā atthakāmaina hitaisinā, anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya| handa mayaṃ, āvusau, tathā vihāraṃ kappaima yathā nau viharataṃ bhagavā attamanau assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau yasaujassa pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau tai bhikkhū vūpakaṭṭhā appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharantā tainaivantaravassaina sabbaiva tissau vij‍jā sacchākaṃsu|

atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina vaisālī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina vaisālī tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā vaggumudātīriyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca manasi karitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘ālaukajātā viya mai, ānanda, aisā disā, aubhāsajātā viya mai, ānanda, aisā disā; yassaṃ disāyaṃ yāyaṃ (ka॰) vaggumudātīriyā bhikkhū  viharanti| gantuṃ appaṭikūlāsi  mai manasi kātuṃ| pahiṇaiyyāsi tvaṃ, ānanda, vaggumudātīriyānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ santikai dūtaṃ – ‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaiti, satthā āyasmantānaṃ dassanakāmau’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, āvusau, yaina vaggumudātīriyā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā vaggumudātīriyai bhikkhū aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaiti, satthā āyasmantānaṃ dassanakāmau’’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau sau bhikkhu āyasmatau ānandassa paṭissutvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ antarahitau vaggumudāya nadiyā tīrai taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ puratau pāturahausi| atha khau sau bhikkhu vaggumudātīriyai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaiti, satthā āyasmantānaṃ dassanakāmau’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – vaggumudāya nadiyā tīrai antarahitā mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ bhagavatau sammukhai pāturahaisuṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā ānaiñ‍jaina samādhinā nisin‍nau  hauti| atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘katamaina nu khau bhagavā vihāraina aitarahi viharatī’’ti? atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ānaiñ‍jaina khau bhagavā vihāraina aitarahi viharatī’’ti| sabbaiva ānaiñ‍jasamādhinā nisīdiṃsu|

atha  khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā, nikkhantai paṭhamai yāmai, uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ cīvaraṃ (sabbattha) karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti; nikkhantau paṭhamau yāmau; ciranisin‍nā āgantukā bhikkhū; paṭisammaudatu, bhantai, bhagavā āgantukaihi bhikkhūhī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi|

dutiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā, nikkhantai majjhimai yāmai, uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti; nikkhantau majjhimau yāmau; ciranisin‍nā āgantukā bhikkhū; paṭisammaudatu, bhantai, bhagavā āgantukaihi bhikkhūhī’’ti| dutiyampi khau bhagavā tuṇhī ahausi|

tatiyampi khau āyasmā ānandau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā, nikkhantai pacchimai yāmai, uddhastai aruṇai, nandimukhiyā rattiyā uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantā, bhantai, ratti; nikkhantau pacchimau yāmau; uddhastau aruṇau; nandimukhī ratti; ciranisin‍nā āgantukā bhikkhū; paṭisammaudatu, bhantai, bhagavā, āgantukaihi  bhikkhūhī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘sacai khau tvaṃ, ānanda, jānaiyyāsi aittakampi tai nappaṭibhāsaiyya nappaṭibhaiyya (?)| ahañ‍ca, ānanda, imāni ca pañ‍ca bhikkhusatāni sabbaiva ānaiñ‍jasamādhinā nisīdimhā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yassa jitau kāmakaṇṭakau,

ak‍kausau ca vadhau ca bandhanañ‍ca|

pabbatauva pabbatau viya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) sau ṭhitau anaijau,

sukhadukkhaisu na vaidhatī sa bhikkhū’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. sāriputtasuttaṃ

24. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ avidūrai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yathāpi  pabbatau sailau, acalau suppatiṭṭhitau|

aivaṃ mauhakkhayā bhikkhu, pabbatauva na vaidhatī’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. mahāmauggal‍lānasuttaṃ

25. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina  āyasmā  mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya kāyagatāya satiyā ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitāya| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ avidūrai nisin‍naṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya kāyagatāya satiyā ajjhattaṃ sūpaṭṭhitāya|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘sati kāyagatā upaṭṭhitā,

chasu phassāyatanaisu saṃvutau|

satataṃ bhikkhu samāhitau,

jañ‍ñā nibbānamattanau’’ti|| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pilindavacchasuttaṃ

26. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā pilindavacchau pilindivacchau (sī॰) bhikkhū vasalavādaina samudācarati| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, pilindavacchau bhikkhū vasalavādaina samudācaratī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina pilindavacchaṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau pilindavaccha vaccha (syā॰), āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā pilindavacchau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  āyasmantaṃ  pilindavacchaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau pilindavaccha, āmantaitī’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā pilindavacchau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ pilindavacchaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, vaccha, bhikkhū vasalavādaina samudācarasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmatau pilindavacchassa pubbainivāsaṃ manasi karitvā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘mā khau tumhai, bhikkhavai, vacchassa bhikkhunau ujjhāyittha| na, bhikkhavai, vacchau dausantarau bhikkhū vasalavādaina samudācarati| vacchassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍ca jātisatāni abbaukiṇṇāni brāhmaṇakulai pac‍cājātāni| sau tassa vasalavādau dīgharattaṃ  samudāciṇṇau ajjhāciṇṇau (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ)| taināyaṃ vacchau bhikkhū vasalavādaina samudācaratī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘yamhī  na māyā vasatī na mānau,

yau vītalaubhau amamau nirāsau|

panuṇṇakaudhau paṇun‍nakaudhau (pī॰) abhinibbutattau,

sau brāhmaṇau sau samaṇau sa bhikkhū’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sak‍kudānasuttaṃ

27. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahākassapau pippaliguhāyaṃ viharati, sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti añ‍ñataraṃ  nisin‍nau añ‍ñataraṃ (syā॰ ka॰) samādhiṃ samāpaj‍jitvā| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhāsi| atha khau āyasmatau mahākassapassa tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhitassa aitadahausi – ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pavisaiyya’’nti|

taina khau pana samayaina pañ‍camattāni daivatāsatāni ussuk‍kaṃ āpan‍nāni haunti āyasmatau  mahākassapassa piṇḍapātapaṭilābhāya| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau tāni pañ‍camattāni daivatāsatāni paṭikkhipitvā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi|

taina khau pana samayaina sak‍kau daivānamindau āyasmatau mahākassapassa piṇḍapātaṃ dātukāmau hauti| paisakāravaṇṇaṃ abhinimminitvā tantaṃ vināti| sujā sujātā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) asurakañ‍ñā tasaraṃ pūraiti| atha khau āyasmā mahākassapau rājagahai sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caramānau yaina sak‍kassa daivānamindassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau sak‍kau daivānamindau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna gharā nikkhamitvā pac‍cugantvā hatthatau pattaṃ gahaitvā gharaṃ pavisitvā pavisaitvā (ka॰) ghaṭiyā audanaṃ uddharitvā pattaṃ pūraitvā āyasmatau mahākassapassa adāsi| sau ahausi piṇḍapātau anaikasūpau anaikabyañ‍janau anaikarasabyañ‍janau anaikasūparasabyañ‍janau (sī॰ pī॰)| atha khau āyasmatau mahākassapassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kau nu khau ayaṃ sattau yassāyaṃ  aivarūpau iddhānubhāvau’’ti? atha khau āyasmatau mahākassapassa aitadahausi – ‘‘sak‍kau khau  ayaṃ daivānamindau’’ti| iti viditvā sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kataṃ khau tai idaṃ, kausiya; mā māssu (sī॰ syā॰) punapi aivarūpamakāsī’’ti| ‘‘amhākampi, bhantai kassapa, puñ‍ñaina atthau; amhākampi puñ‍ñaina karaṇīya’’nti|

atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā ākāsai antalikkhai tikkhattuṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘ahau dānaṃ paramadānaṃ paramaṃ dānaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) kassapai suppatiṭṭhitaṃ! ahau dānaṃ paramadānaṃ kassapai suppatiṭṭhitaṃ!! ahau dānaṃ paramadānaṃ kassapai suppatiṭṭhita’’nti!!! assausi khau bhagavā dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya sak‍kassa daivānamindassa vaihāsaṃ abbhuggantvā ākāsai antalikkhai tikkhattuṃ udānaṃ udānaintassa – ‘‘ahau dānaṃ paramadānaṃ kassapai suppatiṭṭhitaṃ! ahau dānaṃ paramadānaṃ kassapai suppatiṭṭhitaṃ!! ahau dānaṃ paramadānaṃ kassapai suppatiṭṭhita’’nti!!!

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘piṇḍapātikassa bhikkhunau,

attabharassa anañ‍ñapausinau|

daivā  pihayanti tādinau,

upasantassa sadā satīmatau’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. piṇḍapātikasuttaṃ

28. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ karairimaṇḍalamāḷai  san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi –

‘‘piṇḍapātikau, āvusau, bhikkhu piṇḍāya carantau labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai cakkhunā rūpai passituṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai sautaina saddai sautuṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai ghānaina  gandhai ghāyituṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai jivhāya rasai sāyituṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai kāyaina phauṭṭhabbai phusituṃ| piṇḍapātikau, āvusau, bhikkhu sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau piṇḍāya carati| handāvusau, mayampi piṇḍapātikā hauma| mayampi lacchāma kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai cakkhunā rūpai passituṃ, mayampi lacchāma kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai sautaina saddai sautuṃ, mayampi lacchāma kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai ghānaina gandhai ghāyituṃ, mayampi lacchāma kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai jivhāya rasai sāyituṃ, mayampi lacchāma kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai kāyaina phauṭṭhabbai  phusituṃ; mayampi sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā apacitā piṇḍāya carissāmā’’ti| ayañ‍carahi taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā hauti vippakatā|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina karairimaṇḍalamāḷau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ karairimaṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ  san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi –

‘piṇḍapātikau, āvusau, bhikkhu piṇḍāya carantau labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai cakkhunā  rūpai passituṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai sautaina saddai sautuṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai ghānaina gandhai ghāyituṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai jivhāya rasai sāyituṃ, labhati kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai kāyaina phauṭṭhabbai phusituṃ| piṇḍapātikau, āvusau, bhikkhu sak‍katau garukatau mānitau pūjitau apacitau piṇḍāya carati| handāvusau, mayampi piṇḍapātikā hauma| mayampi lacchāma kālaina kālaṃ manāpikai cakkhunā rūpai passituṃ…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbai phusituṃ| mayampi sak‍katā garukatā mānitā pūjitā apacitā piṇḍāya carissāmā’ti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

‘‘na  khvaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ yaṃ tumhai aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ kathaiyyātha| san‍nipatitānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ – dhammī vā kathā ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ  udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘piṇḍapātikassa bhikkhunau,

attabharassa anañ‍ñapausinau|

daivā pihayanti tādinau,

nau cai saddasilaukanissitau’’ti|| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sippasuttaṃ

29. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘kau nu khau, āvusau, sippaṃ jānāti? kau kiṃ sippaṃ sikkhi? kataraṃ sippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti?

tatthaikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘hatthisippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘assasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘rathasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘dhanusippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘tharusippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘muddāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘‘gaṇanāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu  – ‘‘saṅkhānasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘laikhāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kāvaiyyasippaṃ kābyasippaṃ (syā॰) sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘laukāyatasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘khattavij‍jāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’’nti| ayañ‍carahi taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antarākathā hauti vippakatā|

atha khau bhagavā  sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina maṇḍalamāḷau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāya nuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā, kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantānaṃ maṇḍalamāḷai san‍nisin‍nānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘kau nu khau, āvusau, sippaṃ jānāti? kau kiṃ sippaṃ sikkhi? kataraṃ sippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti?

‘‘tatthaikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘hatthisippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti| aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘assasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘rathasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘dhanusippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘tharusippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti, aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘muddāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘gaṇanāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘saṅkhānasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘laikhāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kāvaiyyasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘laukāyatasippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti; aikac‍cai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘khattavij‍jāsippaṃ sippānaṃ agga’nti| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, antarākathā hauti vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti|

‘‘na khvaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ yaṃ tumhai aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ kathaiyyātha| san‍nipatitānaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ – dhammī vā kathā ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘asippajīvī  lahu atthakāmau,

yatindriyau sabbadhi vippamuttau|

anaukasārī amamau nirāsau,

hitvā mānaṃ aikacarau sa bhikkhū’’ti|| navamaṃ|

10. laukasuttaṃ

30. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā uruvailāyaṃ viharati naj‍jā nairañ‍jarāya tīrai baudhirukkhamūlai paṭhamābhisambuddhau| taina khau pana samayaina  bhagavā sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī|

atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaisi| addasā khau bhagavā buddhacakkhunā vaulaukaintau sattai anaikaihi santāpaihi santappamānai, anaikaihi ca pariḷāhaihi pariḍayhamānai – rāgajaihipi, dausajaihipi, mauhajaihipi mauhajaihipīti (sabbattha)|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘ayaṃ laukau santāpajātau,

phassaparaitau raugaṃ vadati attatau|

yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñati yaina hi mañ‍ñati (syā॰ pī॰),

tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā||

‘‘añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau,

bhavaparaitau bhavamaivābhinandati|

yadabhinandati  taṃ bhayaṃ,

yassa bhāyati taṃ dukkhaṃ|

bhavavippahānāya khau panidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vussati’’||

‘‘‘yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhavaina bhavassa vippamaukkhamāhaṃsu, sabbai tai avippamuttā  bhavasmā’ti vadāmi| ‘yai vā pana kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vibhavaina bhavassa nissaraṇamāhaṃsu, sabbai tai anissaṭā bhavasmā’ti vadāmi|

‘‘upadhiñhi paṭic‍ca dukkhamidaṃ sambhauti, sabbupādānakkhayā natthi dukkhassa sambhavau| laukamimaṃ passa; puthū avij‍jāya paraitā bhūtā bhūtaratā aparimuttā; yai hi kaici bhavā sabbadhi sabbatthatāya sabbai tai bhavā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ, sammappañ‍ñāya passatau|

bhavataṇhā pahīyati, vibhavaṃ nābhinandati||

‘‘sabbasau taṇhānaṃ khayā,

asaisavirāganiraudhau nibbānaṃ|

tassa nibbutassa bhikkhunau,

anupādā anupādānā (sī॰) punabbhavau na hauti|

abhibhūtau mārau vijitasaṅgāmau,

upac‍cagā sabbabhavāni tādī’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

nandavaggau tatiyau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kammaṃ  nandau yasaujau ca, sāriputtau ca kaulitau|

pilindau pilindi (sī॰) kassapau piṇḍau, sippaṃ laukaina tai dasāti||


4. maighiyavaggau

1. maighiyasuttaṃ

31. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā cālikāyaṃ viharati cālikai pabbatai| taina  khau pana samayaina āyasmā maighiyau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau āyasmā maighiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, jantugāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisitu’’nti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, maighiya, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā maighiyau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya jantugāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| jantugāmai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina kimikāḷāya nadiyā tīraṃ tainupasaṅkami| upasaṅkamitvā (sabbattha) a॰ ni॰ 9.3 passitabbaṃ addasā  khau āyasmā maighiyau upasaṅkamitvā (sabbattha) a॰ ni॰ 9.3 passitabbaṃ kimikāḷāya nadiyā tīrai jaṅghāvihāraṃ jaṅghavihāraṃ (ka॰) anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau anuvicaramānau addasā khau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), anuvicaramānau addasa (ka॰) ambavanaṃ pāsādikaṃ manuñ‍ñaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘pāsādikaṃ vatidaṃ ambavanaṃ manuñ‍ñaṃ idaṃ padaṃ vidaisapautthakaisu natthi, aṅguttaraipi ramaṇīyaṃ| alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāya| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānaiyya, āgacchaiyyāhaṃ imaṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā maighiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā  bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya jantugāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| jantugāmai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina kimikāḷāya nadiyā tīraṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃ upasaṅkamitvā (sabbattha)| addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai upasaṅkamitvā (sabbattha), kimikāḷāya nadiyā tīrai jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau anuvicaramānau addasaṃ (sabbattha) ambavanaṃ pāsādikaṃ manuñ‍ñaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ| disvāna mai aitadahausi – ‘pāsādikaṃ vatidaṃ ambavanaṃ manuñ‍ñaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ| alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāya| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānaiyya, āgacchaiyyāhaṃ imaṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’ti| sacai maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā anujānāti anujānaiyya (a॰ ni॰ 9.3), gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ  ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā āyasmantaṃ maighiyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaihi tāva, maighiya, aikakamhi aikakamhā (sī॰ pī॰), aikakaumhi (syā॰) tāva, yāva añ‍ñaupi kauci bhikkhu āgacchatī’’ti|

dutiyampi khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavatau, bhantai, natthi kiñ‍ci  uttari uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) karaṇīyaṃ, natthi katassa vā paticayau| mayhaṃ khau pana, bhantai, atthi uttari karaṇīyaṃ, atthi katassa paticayau| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānāti, gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti| dutiyampi khau  bhagavā āyasmantaṃ maighiyaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaihi tāva, maighiya, aikakamhi tāva, yāva añ‍ñaupi kauci bhikkhu āgacchatī’’ti|

tatiyampi khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavatau, bhantai, natthi kiñ‍ci uttari karaṇīyaṃ, natthi katassa vā paticayau| mayhaṃ khau pana, bhantai, atthi uttari karaṇīyaṃ, atthi katassa paticayau| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānāti, gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti| ‘‘padhānanti khau, maighiya, vadamānaṃ kinti vadaiyyāma? yassadāni tvaṃ, maighiya, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā maighiyau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina taṃ ambavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ ambavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā ajjhaugahaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau āyasmatau maighiyassa tasmiṃ ambavanai viharantassa yaibhuyyaina tayau pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā samudācaranti, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau vitak‍kauti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

atha khau āyasmatau maighiyassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! saddhāya ca vatamhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā| atha ca panimaihi tīhi pāpakaihi akusalaihi vitak‍kaihi anvāsattā, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmavitak‍kaina, byāpādavitak‍kaina, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaina’’|

atha khau āyasmā maighiyau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, tasmiṃ ambavanai viharantassa yaibhuyyaina tayau  pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā samudācaranti, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! saddhāya ca vatamhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā| atha ca panimaihi tīhi pāpakaihi akusalaihi  vitak‍kaihi anvāsattā, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmavitak‍kaina, byāpādavitak‍kaina, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaina’’’|

‘‘aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā pañ‍ca dhammā paripākāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca?

‘‘idha, maighiya, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ paṭhamau dhammau paripākāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ dutiyau dhammau paripākāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhu yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā, santuṭṭhikathā, pavivaikakathā, asaṃsaggakathā, vīriyārambhakathā, sīlakathā, samādhikathā, pañ‍ñākathā, vimuttikathā, vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā; aivarūpāya kathāya nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| aparipākāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ tatiyau dhammau paripākāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati, akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya uppādāya (syā॰), thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ catutthau dhammau paripākāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ pañ‍camau dhammau paripākāya saṃvattati| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā imai pañ‍ca dhammā paripākāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa yaṃ sīlavā bhavissati, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharissati, ācāragaucarasampan‍nau, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhissati sikkhāpadaisu|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa yaṃ  yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā aikantanibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya upasamāya abhiñ‍ñāya sambaudhāya nibbānāya saṃvattati, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā, santuṭṭhikathā, pavivaikakathā, asaṃsaggakathā, vīriyārambhakathā, sīlakathā, samādhikathā, pañ‍ñākathā, vimuttikathā, vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā; aivarūpāya kathāya nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa yaṃ āraddhavīriyau viharissati  akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa yaṃ pañ‍ñavā bhavissati, udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā|

‘‘taina ca pana, maighiya, bhikkhunā imaisu pañ‍casu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya cattārau dhammā uttari bhāvaitabbā – asubhā bhāvaitabbā rāgassa pahānāya, maittā bhāvaitabbā byāpādassa pahānāya, ānāpānassati bhāvaitabbā vitak‍kupacchaidāya, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā asmimānasamugghātāya| anic‍casañ‍ñinau hi, maighiya, anattasañ‍ñā saṇṭhāti, anattasañ‍ñī asmimānasamugghātaṃ pāpuṇāti diṭṭhaiva dhammai nibbāna’’nti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘khuddā  vitak‍kā sukhumā vitak‍kā,

anugatā anuggatā (sī॰ ka॰ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) manasau uppilāvā ubbilāpā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

aitai avidvā manasau vitak‍kai,

hurā huraṃ dhāvati bhantacittau||

‘‘aitai ca vidvā manasau vitak‍kai,

ātāpiyau saṃvaratī satīmā|

anugatai  manasau uppilāvai,

asaisamaitai pajahāsi buddhau’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. uddhatasuttaṃ

32. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kusinārāyaṃ viharati upavattanai mal‍lānaṃ sālavanai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavatau avidūrai arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ viharanti uddhatā un‍naḷā capalā mukharā vikiṇṇavācā muṭṭhassatinau asampajānā asamāhitā vibbhantacittā pākatindriyā|

addasā khau bhagavā tai sambahulai  bhikkhū avidūrai arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ viharantai uddhatai un‍naḷai capalai mukharai vikiṇṇavācai muṭṭhassatinau asampajānai asamāhitai vibbhantacittai pākatindriyai|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘arakkhitaina kāyaina cittaina (naittiyaṃ), micchādiṭṭhihataina micchādiṭṭhigataina (bahūsu) ca|

thinamiddhā thīnamiddhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhibhūtaina, vasaṃ mārassa gacchati||

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau|

sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbayaṃ|

thīnamiddhābhibhū bhikkhu, sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti|| dutiyaṃ|

3. gaupālakasuttaṃ

33. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ carati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| atha khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma yaina añ‍ñataraṃ rukkhamūlaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau gaupālakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ gaupālakaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samādāpaisi (?) samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi|

atha khau sau gaupālakau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samādipitau (?) samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘adhivāsaitu mai, bhantai, bhagavā svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau sau gaupālakau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau sau gaupālakau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sakai nivaisanai pahūtaṃ appaudakapāyasaṃ appaudakapāyāsaṃ (sabbattha) paṭiyādāpaitvā navañ‍ca sappiṃ bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhantai, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina yaina tassa gaupālakassa nivaisanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| atha khau sau gaupālakau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ appaudakapāyasaina appaudakapāyāsaina ca (syā॰ pī॰) navaina ca sappinā sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau sau gaupālakau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ  āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau taṃ gaupālakaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau taṃ gaupālakaṃ añ‍ñatarau  purisau sīmantarikāya jīvitā vauraupaisi|

atha  khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yaina, bhantai, gaupālakaina aj‍ja buddhappamukhau bhikkhusaṅghau appaudakapāyasaina navaina ca sappinā sahatthā santappitau sampavāritau sau kira, bhantai, gaupālakau añ‍ñataraina purisaina sīmantarikāya jīvitā vauraupitau’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā aitamatthaṃ viditvā tāyaṃ vailāyaṃ imaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi –

‘‘disau disaṃ yaṃ taṃ kayirā, vairī vā pana vairinaṃ|

micchāpaṇihitaṃ cittaṃ, pāpiyau naṃ tatau karai’’ti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. yakkhapahārasuttaṃ

34. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau kapautakandarāyaṃ viharanti| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau juṇhāya rattiyā navauraupitaihi kaisaihi abbhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti añ‍ñataraṃ samādhiṃ samāpaj‍jitvā|

taina khau pana samayaina dvai yakkhā sahāyakā uttarāya disāya dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ gacchanti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| addasaṃsu khau tai yakkhā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ juṇhāya rattiyā navauraupitaihi kaisaihi abbhaukāsai nisin‍naṃ| disvāna aikau yakkhau dutiyaṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, samma, imassa samaṇassa sīsai pahāraṃ dātu’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, sau yakkhau taṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, samma, mā samaṇaṃ āsādaisi| uḷārau sau, samma, samaṇau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’’ti|

dutiyampi khau sau yakkhau taṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, samma, imassa samaṇassa  sīsai pahāraṃ dātu’’nti| dutiyampi khau sau yakkhau taṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, samma, mā samaṇaṃ āsādaisi| uḷārau sau, samma, samaṇau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’’ti| tatiyampi khau sau yakkhau taṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṭibhāti maṃ, samma, imassa samaṇassa sīsai pahāraṃ  dātu’’nti| tatiyampi khau sau yakkhau taṃ yakkhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, samma, mā samaṇaṃ āsādaisi| uḷārau sau, samma, samaṇau mahiddhikau mahānubhāvau’’ti|

atha khau sau yakkhau taṃ yakkhaṃ anādiyitvā āyasmatau sāriputtatthairassa sīsai pahāraṃ adāsi| tāva mahā pahārau ahausi, api taina pahāraina sattaratanaṃ vā aḍḍhaṭṭhamaratanaṃ vā nāgaṃ ausādaiyya, mahantaṃ vā pabbatakūṭaṃ padālaiyya| atha ca pana sau yakkhau ‘ḍayhāmi ḍayhāmī’ti vatvā tatthaiva mahānirayaṃ apatāsi avatthāsi (ka॰ sī॰)|

addasā khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina taina yakkhaina āyasmatau sāriputtatthairassa sīsai pahāraṃ dīyamānaṃ| disvā yaina āyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci  tai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci na kiñ‍ci dukkha’’nti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ mai, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, yāpanīyaṃ mai, āvusau mauggal‍lāna; api ca mai sīsaṃ thaukaṃ dukkha’’nti|
